# How this repository was made (Chile)

This file is written by the build on 2026-11-02, the day the source data was retrieved.

## Sources

| Source | Address | Retrieved | Licence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| LeyChile (Biblioteca del Congreso Nacional de Chile) | <https://www.bcn.cl/leychile> | 2026-10-28 | Official text published by BCN LeyChile; attribution to BCN. Ley 17.336 has no explicit exclusion for legal texts (art. 88 vests works of state officials in the State): licence status to confirm. |
| Diario Oficial de la República de Chile (Ministerio del Interior y Seguridad Pública) | <https://www.diariooficial.interior.gob.cl/publicaciones/2018/01/20/41962/01/1338367.pdf> | 2026-11-02 | Official gazette of the Republic of Chile; Ley 21.057 as published on 2018-01-20 (edition 41.962, CVE 1338367). Attribution to the Diario Oficial. |

Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

The build made 3405 commits:

| Commits | Kind |
| ---: | --- |
| 382 | first version of an act |
| 2464 | one law on its in-force day |
| 559 | changes no amendment note explains, credited to the publisher |

Other counts:
- 17 commits on the branch `futuro`: laws not yet in force on 2026-11-02.
- 95 commits dated 1 January 1970: in force before 1970, with the real day in their `In-force` trailer.
- 2527 laws in the record.
- 7034 tags.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (America/Santiago) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Código Civil art. 7: "La publicación de la ley se hará mediante su inserción en el Diario Oficial, y desde la fecha de éste se entenderá conocida de todos y será obligatoria." A law's own rule on its vigencia overrides this (art. 7, inciso tercero). https://www.bcn.cl/leychile/navegar?idNorma=172986&idParte=8717776

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `6a40e0282cb5443ba841674f4d655aeb8db7521ac15ae4ffe0a8af5bff3cfa27` (131702819 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `a4faa25af85facb64b956b372f6b528aafdd7ec1`, `check.py` `3d00541d258745461ab52b1b0cb4e1716a27a95d`, `on.py` `644a6c0857fbcc72562032b58d57aa113fee0641`.
- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

## Checks

4622 versions of 382 acts read back from git on the day each took effect and on the day before: 0 mismatches. Trailers: 0 commits whose trailers git cannot parse. Health: 0 merge commits on main, 0 commit dates that go backwards, 0 commits dated after the build.

No second build of this corpus has been compared yet.

## Decisions made by hand

- Sources: LeyChile JSON services get_versiones (the dated versions and the norms that ended each one) and get_norma_json (the text of each version). The legacy XML export (Consulta/obtxml) still answered 200 on 2026-10-28, but its notes are laid out in a second text column mixed into the lines, so the JSON was used.
- In-force dates: each version starts on LeyChile's "vigenteDesde", which LeyChile sets to the amending norm's "inicioVigencia". Where the norm has no vigencia clause that is its publication day in the Diario Oficial (Código Civil art. 7); where it has one (for example Ley 21.504, published 2022-11-10, in force 2023-05-09), LeyChile uses that day. We take LeyChile's day as given.
- Credits come only from the source: a changed unit is credited to a norm only if that norm took effect on the version's day (per get_versiones) and a note on the unit names it (by link, by type and number, or a Rectificación by its Diario Oficial date). A unit removed outright is credited to the norm that took effect that day only when exactly one did. Everything else is unexplained and committed by the publisher.
- A texto refundido (a norm that re-enacts an act, e.g. DS 100 of 2005 for the Constitution) is the act's next version from its first day: every unit that differs is credited to that norm, and renumbered provisions (paired by at least half of their words shared) are listed in the commit message.
- One file per leaf of LeyChile's table of contents (article, transitional provision, heading, closing); one line per paragraph; the headings of books, titles, chapters and paragraphs are kept, in order, in `titulos.md`. Amendment notes are removed from the text; NBSP, quotes and dashes are normalised.
- Rectificaciones (corrections published in the Diario Oficial) are commits of kind "decree", credited like a norm.
- CPR: The Constitution of 1980 (DL 3.464, published in the Diario Oficial on 11 August 1980) was approved by plebiscite on 11 September 1980 and, by its own Artículo final, "entrará en vigencia seis meses después de ser aprobada mediante plebiscito", that is on 11 March 1981. Its first version is dated 1981-03-11, not the publication day LeyChile shows. The Artículo final makes the ninth and twenty-third transitional provisions apply from the plebiscite; that is in the text, not in the dates.
- CPR: Art. 26 takes LeyChile's wording with Ley 19.643's change on 1999-11-05, not on 1999-11-04 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 19.643's own text gives that day (Ley 19.643, published 1999-11-05, has no clause of its own on when its change to art. 26 takes effect (its artículo transitorio defers only the rules on the integration of the Tribunal Calificador de Elecciones), so that change is in force from its publication; LeyChile's one-day version of 1999-11-04 dates the law "D.O. 04.11.1999", and its next version "D.O. 05.11.1999"); LeyChile's 1999-11-04, before the law was published, is a date error.
- CPR: Art. 84 keeps its earlier text from 1999-11-05 to 2000-01-31; Ley 19.643's new wording is dated 2000-01-31 by the law's own text (Ley 19.643, artículo transitorio: "Las normas referidas a la integración del Tribunal Calificador de Elecciones regirán a partir del 31 de enero de año 2.000."), and LeyChile dates it 1999-11-05.
- CPR: Art. 109 keeps its earlier text from 2020-08-20 to 2020-09-08; Ley 21.253's new wording is dated 2020-09-08 by the law's own text (Ley 21.253, disposición cuadragésima transitoria: "La reforma constitucional al artículo 109 empezará a regir una vez que entre en vigencia la ley que introduce modificaciones a la ley N° 18.840, Orgánica Constitucional del Banco Central de Chile, que regulará el ejercicio de la nueva facultad que se le otorga al Banco Central"; that law, Ley 21.265, was published and took effect on 2020-09-08), and LeyChile dates it 2020-08-20.
- CPR: art. 110 in the words Ley 19.526 gave it (art. 121 from 2005-09-22, DS 100 of 2005) keeps its day, 1997-11-17. Disposición trigésima octava transitoria (décima from 2005-09-22) makes the municipalities' powers over their structure, staff and pay "aplicables cuando se regulen en la ley respectiva las modalidades, requisitos y limitaciones para el ejercicio de estas nuevas competencias". That is read as the article's own reference to the ley orgánica constitucional de municipalidades, whose "límites y requisitos" its second inciso already requires, not as a hold on the constitutional text, which is in force from the reform's publication.
- Código Penal, Código de Comercio, Código del Trabajo, Código de Procedimiento Civil and Código Procesal Penal are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-28 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions: CP 1984, CCom 1974, CT 3471 (DFL 1 of 1994) then 207436 (DFL 1 of 2003), CPC 22740 (Ley 1.552), CPP 176595 (Ley 19.696).
- Código del Trabajo: LeyChile keeps the Código del Trabajo of 1987 (Ley 18.620, idNorma 30011) in one undated version only, so its history from 1987 to 1994 is not available; the act starts with the texto refundido of DFL 1 of 1994 as the oldest version available, and DFL 1 of 2003 is its next text.
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: CCom from 1988-10-20 (14 older versions affected); CPC from 2004-08-30 (29 older versions affected). The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.
- CPP: The Código Procesal Penal (Ley 19.696, published in the Diario Oficial on 12 October 2000) says in its own art. 484 when it takes effect: "regirá para las Regiones de Coquimbo y de la Araucanía desde el 16 de diciembre de 2000", and for the other regions from 16 October 2001, 2002 and 2003 (dates later changed by amendment; today's text has the Región Metropolitana from 16 June 2005). Its first version is dated 2000-12-16, the first day it was in force anywhere, not the publication day LeyChile shows; the regional phases are in the text of art. 484, not in the dates.
- CP: provisions whose LeyChile text in a version carries notes naming a norm published after that version (rule: correct only those provisions, never keep text in force before its law; placeholder "[La fuente no da el texto vigente en esta fecha.]" credited to the publisher): Art. 367 1977-12-14 to 1996-03-08 placeholder; from 1997-05-17 credited to Ley 19.450; Párrafo 2014-09-17 to 2020-01-30 placeholder; from 2020-03-04 credited to Ley 21.212.
- CT: provisions whose LeyChile text in a version carries notes naming a norm published after that version (rule: correct only those provisions, never keep text in force before its law; placeholder "[La fuente no da el texto vigente en esta fecha.]" credited to the publisher): Art. 25 1994-01-26 to 2001-07-06 placeholder; from 2001-10-05 credited to Ley 19.759; Art. 168 1994-01-25 to 1995-02-18 placeholder; from 1996-02-08 credited to Ley 19.447; Art. 170 1994-01-25 to 1995-02-18 placeholder; from 1996-02-08 credited to Ley 19.447; Art. 415 2003-01-16 to 2007-11-15 placeholder; from 2008-03-29 credited to Ley 20.252; Art. 416 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 417 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 418 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 419 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 421 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 422 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 423 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 424 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022; Art. 439 bis 2009-10-30 to 2014-08-12 placeholder; Art. 480 1994-01-25 to 1995-02-18 placeholder; from 1996-02-08 credited to Ley 19.447; Capítulo 2010-02-10 to 2017-06-09 placeholder; from 2017-06-20 credited to Ley 21.015; Títulos 2003-01-16 to 2005-03-18 placeholder; from 2005-05-30 credited to Ley 20.022.
- CPP: provisions whose LeyChile text in a version carries notes naming a norm published after that version (rule: correct only those provisions, never keep text in force before its law; placeholder "[La fuente no da el texto vigente en esta fecha.]" credited to the publisher): Parágrafo 2011-08-13 to 2023-05-23 placeholder; from 2023-06-15 credited to Ley 21.577.
- CP: art-021, art-049, art-049-bis, art-049-ter, art-049-quarter, art-049-quinquies, art-049-sexies take their ley-20587 wording on 2013-12-27, the day the amending law's own vigencia clause gives (Ley 20.587, art. 5: arts. 2 and 3 "entrarán a regir el día en que se publique en el Diario Oficial el reglamento" of the Ministerio de Justicia; Decreto 552 of 2013 (Justicia), the reglamento de la pena de prestación de servicios en beneficio de la comunidad, was published on 2013-12-27), not on 2012-06-08 as LeyChile dates it; the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- CT: 1 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 20.899's change on 2016-02-08, not on 2016-01-01 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 20.899 applies it from 2016-01-01, before its publication (Ley 20.899, published 2016-02-08, art. 10: "Modifícase, a contar del 1 de enero de 2016, el inciso primero del artículo 48 del Código del Trabajo"); publication-day rule: a law's change takes effect no earlier than its publication, its earlier legal start recorded here.
- CP: 8 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 19.335's change on 1994-12-24, not on 1993-12-24 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 19.335's own text gives that day (Ley 19.335, published 1994-09-23, art. 37: "Esta ley entrará en vigencia, con excepción de lo dispuesto en los números 8 y 9 de su artículo 28, transcurridos 3 meses desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial" (art. 28 amends the Código Civil); three months from publication end on 1994-12-23 (Código Civil art. 48), so the law is in force from 1994-12-24, and LeyChile's 1993-12-24 has the year wrong); LeyChile's 1993-12-24, before the law was published, is a date error.
- CPP: The changes of Ley 21.057 art. 32 (art. 78 bis inciso 3 and art. 191 bis repealed, the new art. 110 bis, arts. 280 and 310) take effect on 2019-10-03, the first day the law applied in any region of its regional phase-in (Ley 21.057 (published 2018-01-20), artículo primero transitorio: "La presente ley comenzará a regir en forma gradual [...]: Primera etapa: entrará en vigencia transcurridos seis meses después de publicado en el Diario Oficial el reglamento a que alude el artículo 29"; the reglamento, Decreto 471 of 2018 (Justicia y Derechos Humanos), was published on 2019-04-02, and the six months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2019-10-02), not on 2022-10-03 as LeyChile dates the text (the last regional group); each holds one wording for the whole country, and carries LeyChile's wording of 2022-10-03..
- CT: the 2003-11-01 version (Art. 145 a, Art. 145 b, Art. 145 c, Art. 145 d, Art. 145 e, Art. 145 f, Art. 145 g, Art. 145 h, Art. 145 i, Art. 145 j, Art. 145 k and Art. 146) is credited to Ley 19.889: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 19.889, and its artículo 1° transitorio ("La presente ley entrará en vigencia el día 1 del mes subsiguiente al de su publicación en el Diario Oficial") puts these changes in force on that day.
- CT: the 2018-04-01 version (Art. 157 bis, Art. 157 ter and Títulos) is credited to Ley 21.015: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.015, and its artículo primero transitorio ("La presente ley entrará en vigencia el primer día del mes subsiguiente a la publicación en el Diario Oficial de los reglamentos señalados en sus artículos [...]") puts these changes in force on the first day of the second month after its reglamentos, Decretos 64 and 65 of 2017 (Trabajo y Previsión Social), published 2018-02-01: that day.
- CT: the 2025-01-01 version (Art. 157 sexies) is credited to Ley 21.690: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.690, and its artículo cuarto transitorio ("El artículo 157 sexies del Código del Trabajo, incorporado por el artículo 1°, número 7, de la presente ley, entrará en vigencia el año siguiente a la fecha de publicación de esta ley") puts this change in force in the year after its publication (2024-08-24), from its first day.
- CP: the 1997-05-17 version (121 provisions, Art. 90 to Art. 490, whose fines in sueldos vitales the table converts) is credited to Ley 19.450: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 19.450, and its art. 1 ("Sustitúyense las escalas de multas establecidas en el Código Penal, que estén señaladas en sueldos vitales o en fracciones de sueldo vital, por otras expresadas en unidades tributarias mensuales o fracción de unidad tributaria mensual, de acuerdo con la siguiente tabla de conversión [...] En consecuencia, cada vez que en un determinado artículo del Código Penal aparezca consignada alguna escala de multas en sueldos vitales o en fracciones de sueldo vital, deberá reemplazarse por la que corresponda en unidades tributarias mensuales, de acuerdo con la tabla de conversión establecida en este artículo") converts these fines by its table (as Ley 19.501, published 1997-05-15, worded it before it took effect), and Ley 19.456 (published 1996-05-16), artículo único ("El plazo de entrada en vigencia de la ley N° 19.450, será de un año, a contar de la publicación de esta ley en el Diario Oficial"), puts it in force one year after that publication: the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48, ends on 1997-05-16, so on that day. Each of the 121 provisions was checked against Ley 19.450's own text: its change is the table's row for its earlier fines, the row its new LeyChile note names. Art. 265, credited too since its 1977-12-14 text reads as DL 2.059 worded it, is the table's rows i) and d).
- CT: art. 154 takes Ley 20.348's wording (art. 1 N° 2 a)) on 2009-12-20, not on 2009-12-19 as LeyChile dates it (uncredited there): Ley 20.348 (published 2009-06-19), artículo transitorio: "Lo dispuesto en la letra a. del número 2 del artículo 1°, comenzará a regir seis meses después de su publicación en el Diario Oficial"; the six months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2009-12-19; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. Credited to Ley 20.348.
- CT: the changes of Ley 21.561 art. 1 that its artículo primero transitorio puts in force one year after publication (arts. 22, 25 bis, 25 ter, 27, 32, 33, 34 bis, 38, 40 bis, 109, 149, 150, 152 quáter J and the repeal of art. 375) take effect on 2024-04-27, not on 2024-04-26 as LeyChile dates them (uncredited there): Ley 21.561 (published 2023-04-26), artículo primero transitorio: the changes "referidas al inciso segundo del artículo 22; al inciso primero del artículo 25 bis y al número 5 del artículo 25 ter; al artículo 27; al inciso cuarto del artículo 32; al artículo 33; al inciso primero del artículo 34 bis; a los incisos quinto y séptimo del artículo 38; al artículo 40 bis; al inciso primero del artículo 109; a la letra d) del artículo 149; a la letra d) del artículo 150; al inciso cuarto del artículo 152 quáter J, y a la derogación del artículo 375 [...] comenzarán a regir en el plazo de un año contado desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial"; the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48, ends on 2024-04-26; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. LeyChile dates the same year's change to Ley 19.518 2024-04-27 (99c-batch12). Credited to Ley 21.561.
- CT: the changes of Ley 21.561 art. 1 to the working day that its artículo primero transitorio N° 2 applies in the fifth year (arts. 25, 25 ter, 26 bis, 28, 38, 88 and 152 ter D) take effect on 2028-04-27, not on 2028-04-26 as LeyChile dates them (uncredited there): Ley 21.561 (published 2023-04-26), artículo primero transitorio, N° 2: "La regulación relativa a la jornada contenida en el artículo 1, referidas al inciso primero del artículo 25; al inciso primero del artículo 25 bis; al número 1 del artículo 25 ter; al artículo 26 bis; al inciso primero del artículo 28; al inciso octavo e inciso final del artículo 38; al inciso segundo del artículo 88 y al inciso primero del artículo 152 ter D, se aplicará al quinto año de la publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the five years, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2028-04-26; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. Art. 25 bis inciso primero, as Ley 21.755 art. 4 replaced it, moves with them: Ley 21.755 (published 2025-07-11) art. 5 applies it "con la gradualidad establecida en el numeral 2 del artículo primero transitorio de la ley N° 21.561"; it stays credited to Ley 21.755. Credited to Ley 21.561.
- CT: art. 22 inciso primero takes Ley 21.561's wording (forty hours a week, distributed by calendar week or on weekly averages over up to four weeks) on 2028-04-27, the fifth-year step of the law's artículo primero transitorio, not on its publication, 2023-04-26, as LeyChile dates it (coordinator decision, the effect-day rule): Ley 21.561 (published 2023-04-26), artículo primero transitorio: "Esta ley entrará en vigencia en forma gradual, según las modalidades y plazos siguientes" [...] "1. La regulación relativa a las horas de trabajo contenidas en el artículo 1, referidas al inciso primero del artículo 22; [...] se reducirá a cuarenta y cuatro horas al primer año; cuarenta y dos horas al tercer año y cuarenta horas al quinto año, contados desde la publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the five years, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2028-04-26; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. The transitory article brings the reduction in by steps; it does not make the new inciso apply at once with temporary limits, so the earlier inciso primero (forty-five hours) stands until then. The intermediate steps, forty-four hours from 2024-04-27 (the first year) and forty-two hours from 2026-04-27 (the third year), exist only in the transitory article and change no words of the Code. The rest of art. 22 keeps LeyChile's days: the repeal of inciso cuarto on 2023-04-26 and the new inciso segundo on 2024-04-27 (the one-year list). Credited to Ley 21.561.
- CP: from 1977-12-14 to 1997-05-16, art. 265's two fines read "once a veinte sueldos vitales" (when the desacato is grave) and "seis a diez sueldos vitales" (when it is leve), as DL 2.059 (published 1977-12-14), art. 1 N° 27: "Artículo 265.- Se sustituyen las frases 'sesenta a seiscientos escudos' y 'sesenta a ciento ochenta escudos', por 'once a veinte sueldos vitales' y 'seis a diez sueldos vitales', respectivamente". LeyChile's text from 1977-12-14 gives "seis a diez sueldos vitales" for the first fine and keeps the second in pesos ("ciento a trescientos pesos"), which the decree replaced: a consolidation slip, and the text of the decree governs over the publisher's print. With the decree's words, Ley 19.450 art. 1's conversion table (rows i) and d), the rows LeyChile's 1997 notes name) gives exactly LeyChile's text from 1997-05-17, so that day's change to art. 265 is credited to Ley 19.450 as well (coordinator decision: fixed only with a sourced, dated change). The decree replaced fines in escudos ("sesenta a seiscientos escudos", "sesenta a ciento ochenta escudos"), where LeyChile's text before 1977-12-14, here and in the Code's other fines, still gives the pesos of 1874: the law that turned them into escudos is not named in the sources this record uses, so the earlier text is kept as LeyChile gives it.
- CT: art. 22 bis (the ordinary week distributed on a weekly average of forty hours in cycles of up to four weeks), inserted by Ley 21.561 art. 1, is not in the Code until then and takes effect on 2028-04-27, the fifth-year step of Ley 21.561's artículo primero transitorio N° 1, which brings in the forty hours its wording states, not on its publication, 2023-04-26, as LeyChile dates it (coordinator decision: the reading of art. 22): Ley 21.561 (published 2023-04-26), artículo primero transitorio N° 1: the hours of the provisions it names "se reducirá a cuarenta y cuatro horas al primer año; cuarenta y dos horas al tercer año y cuarenta horas al quinto año, contados desde la publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. The forty-four and forty-two hours of the first and third years (2024-04-27, 2026-04-27) exist only in the transitory article and change no words of the Code. Credited to Ley 21.561.

The forty-four and forty-two hours of the first and third years (2024-04-27, 2026-04-27) exist only in the transitory article and change no words of the Code. - CP: before 1977-12-14 the Code's fines are given as LeyChile gives them, in the pesos of its 1874 text. The amounts in escudos that stood in the Code before DL 2.059 (published 1977-12-14) replaced them with sueldos vitales are not shown, because the law that converted the fines into escudos could not be identified in the sources this record uses (coordinator decision: LeyChile's text is kept as it is).
- CT: art. 31 inciso primero's last sentence (with the art. 22 bis modality, ordinary and overtime hours together may not exceed fifty-two a week), added by Ley 21.561 art. 1, takes effect on 2028-04-27, not on its publication, 2023-04-26, as LeyChile dates it: the sentence governs only the modality of art. 22 bis, which takes effect that day (the fifth-year step of the law's artículo primero transitorio N° 1; see its own line), so it is held to the same day (coordinator decision). Until then art. 31 inciso primero reads as before. Credited to Ley 21.561.
- CCom: the 2022-06-07 version (art. 538 bis, added by Ley 21.314 art. 8 N° 2) keeps LeyChile's day, uncredited: Ley 21.314 (published 2021-04-13), artículo noveno transitorio: "Las modificaciones que introduce el artículo 538 bis contenido en el artículo 8º de esta ley comenzarán a regir sesenta días después de dictada la respectiva norma de carácter general por la Comisión para el Mercado Financiero". The norm that fixes that day could not be read from the sources this record uses, so the day is not checked against the law's own text.

- Código Tributario, Código Sanitario, Código de Aguas, Ley 18.046 and Ley 19.496 are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-28 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions: DL 830 of 1974 (6374), DFL 725 of 1968 (5595), DFL 1.122 of 1981 (5605), Ley 18.046 (29473), and Ley 19.496 (61438) followed by its texto refundido, DFL 3 of 2021 (1160403).
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.

- Ley 19.496: first version dated 1997-06-06, the day its own vigencia clause gives ("La presente ley entrará en vigencia noventa días después de su publicación en el Diario Oficial" (art. 1 transitorio; published 1997-03-07)), not the publication day LeyChile uses; the 1 LeyChile version(s) dated before it are folded into it (Código Civil art. 48 for the count).
- CTrib: Art. 4 ter take LeyChile's wording on 2024-11-13, the publication of Ley 21.716, instead of the placeholder line until 2025-01-01: the law applies it from 2024-11-01, before it was published (Ley 21.716, published 2024-11-13, artículo transitorio: "La presente ley entrará en vigencia el 31 de octubre de 2024"), and retroactive effects keep the publication day; LeyChile's text of these provisions is the same on 2024-11-01 and 2025-01-01.
- Ley 19.496: The words of art. 50 C, inciso primero, on free legal aid by the Corporaciones de Asistencia Judicial ("En caso de que el consumidor no cuente con los medios para costear su defensa, será asistido por la Corporación de Asistencia Judicial correspondiente.") are left out of the text: they take effect on a day no published norm has fixed yet (Ley 21.081 (published 2018-09-13), artículo noveno transitorio: "La parte final del inciso primero del artículo 50 C de la ley N° 19.496, en lo que corresponde a la asistencia jurídica gratuita de las Corporaciones de Asistencia Judicial [...] entrará en vigencia una vez que esté en funcionamiento el servicio público de asistencia judicial que sucederá a las actuales Corporaciones de Asistencia Judicial"; that service, the Servicio Nacional de Acceso a la Justicia y Defensoría de Víctimas of Ley 21.780 (published 2025-11-13), has not started: LeyChile gives Ley 21.780 as waiting on that event as of 2026-11-01), and provisions waiting on an undated condition stay out until it is dated; LeyChile gives them from 2019-09-14. Until then the same article funds that aid through agreements between the Ministries of Economy and Justice. The other provisions keep LeyChile's day.
- CTrib: The changes of Ley 20.322 artículo segundo (the Tribunales Tributarios y Aduaneros: arts. 6, 24, 25, 54, 59 bis, 64 and 105 to 165 as amended, and the headings of Libro Tercero, its Título I and Título III Párrafo 2°) take effect on 2010-02-02, the first day the law applied in any region of its regional phase-in (Ley 20.322 (published 2009-01-27), artículo 1° transitorio: "Los artículos Primero a Noveno y Undécimo de esta ley entrarán en vigencia en cuatro años contados desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial. Sin perjuicio de lo dispuesto en el inciso anterior, dichos artículos entrarán a regir: En un año, contado desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las siguientes Regiones: XV Región de Arica y Parinacota, I Región de Tarapacá, II Región de Antofagasta, III Región de Atacama"; the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48 from 2009-02-01, ends on 2010-02-01), not on 2013-02-01 as LeyChile dates the text (the last regional group); each holds one wording for the whole country, and carries LeyChile's wording of 2013-02-01; in titulos only the lines it replaces (3), as other laws change that unit in between..
- CTrib: the 2011-03-28 version (Art. 8 ter) is credited to Ley 20.494: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 20.494, and its artículo primero transitorio ("Lo dispuesto en el artículo 8° ter que introduce al Código Tributario el artículo 2° de esta ley entrará en vigencia en el plazo de dos meses contado desde su publicación"; published 2011-01-27, the two months run to the end of 2011-03-27, Código Civil art. 48) puts this change in force on that day.
- CTrib: the 2015-09-30 version (Art. 4 bis, Art. 4 quáter, Art. 4 quinquies, Art. 4 ter, Art. 6, Art. 8, Art. 11, Art. 13, Art. 17, Art. 21, Art. 26 bis, Art. 35, Art. 59, Art. 59 bis, Art. 60, Art. 60 bis, Art. 60 quáter, Art. 60 quinquies, Art. 60 ter, Art. 68, Art. 69, Art. 84 bis, Art. 85, Art. 88, Art. 97, Art. 100 bis, Art. 119, Art. 160 bis, Art. 165, Art. 169, Art. 171, Art. 185, Art. 192, Art. 195 and Títulos) is credited to Ley 20.780: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 20.780, and its artículo decimoquinto transitorio ("Las modificaciones al Código Tributario regirán transcurrido un año desde la publicación de la ley"; published 2014-09-29, the year runs to the end of 2015-09-29, Código Civil art. 48; LeyChile's page for this day carries a new Ley 20.780 art. 10 note on every changed provision) puts these changes in force on that day.
- CTrib: the 2025-01-01 version (Art. 3, Art. 53, Art. 55, Art. 92 ter, Art. 130, Art. 131 bis, Art. 161, Art. 169, Art. 170, Art. 175, Art. 176, Art. 180 and Art. 197 bis) is credited to Ley 21.713: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.713, and its artículo primero transitorio, N° 2, 7, 9 and 11 (the changes to arts. 3, 53 and 55, the new art. 92 ter, the changes to arts. 130, 131 bis and 161, and those to arts. 169, 170 inciso primero, 175, 176, 180 and the new art. 197 bis "entrarán en vigencia a partir del 1 de enero de 2025" or "comenzarán a regir a partir del 1° de enero de 2025") puts these changes in force on that day.
- CTrib: the 2025-04-25 version (Art. 100 quáter, Art. 100 quinquies and Art. 100 ter) is credited to Ley 21.713: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.713, and its artículo primero transitorio, N° 8 ("los nuevos artículos 100 ter, 100 quáter y 100 quinquies, entrarán en vigencia seis meses después de la publicación en el Diario Oficial de la presente ley"; published 2024-10-24, the six months run to the end of 2025-04-24, Código Civil art. 48) puts these changes in force on that day.
- CTrib: the 2025-05-01 version (Art. 9 and Art. 11) is credited to Ley 21.713: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.713, and its artículo primero transitorio, N° 3 ("Las modificaciones incorporadas en los artículos 9 y 11 entrarán en vigencia a partir del 1 de mayo de 2025") puts these changes in force on that day.
- CTrib: the 2025-07-01 version (Art. 89) is credited to Ley 21.713: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.713, and its artículo primero transitorio, N° 6 ("Las modificaciones al artículo 89 comenzarán a regir a contar del 1 de julio de 2025") puts this change in force on that day.
- CAguas: the 2025-04-06 version (Art. 132) is credited to Ley 21.435: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.435, and its artículo décimo transitorio, as Ley 21.586 art. 1 N° 2 worded it ("Lo dispuesto en el inciso primero del artículo 132 comenzará a regir desde el 6 de abril de 2025") puts this change in force on that day.
- CAguas: the 2026-02-16 version (Art. 307 ter) is credited to Ley 21.770: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.770, and its artículo décimo noveno transitorio ("lo dispuesto en el número 23 del artículo 85 entrará en vigencia con la publicación en el Diario Oficial de la resolución de la Dirección General de Aguas a la que se refiere dicha disposición") puts this change in force on the publication of that resolution: Resolución 281 exenta of 2026 (Dirección General de Aguas), which "determina los contenidos mínimos que deberán contener los informes técnicos de pre revisión suscritos por profesionales o entidades", published on that day.
- CTrib: the 2025-05-01 version (Art. 6 bis) is credited to Ley 21.713: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.713, and its artículo primero transitorio, N° 1 ("El nuevo artículo 6° bis, que incorpora el numeral 7 del artículo 1 de la presente ley, comenzará a regir una vez constituido el Consejo Tributario, según lo establecido en el artículo vigesimotercero transitorio"; the article meant is the vigesimosegundo, which says the Consejo "se constituirá el primer día del séptimo mes siguiente al de la publicación de la presente ley, fecha en que entrarán en vigencia ambas disposiciones") puts this change in force on that day, the day the law itself gives for the Consejo's constitution; Decreto 148 exento of 2025 (Hacienda, published 2025-06-11), which designates the four Consejeros "a contar del 2 de mayo de 2025", does not change the law's own day (coordinator decision).
- CTrib: arts. 171, 174, 178, 179 and 185 take Ley 21.713's wording on 2024-12-25, not on 2025-01-24 as LeyChile dates it (uncredited there): Ley 21.713 (published 2024-10-24), artículo primero transitorio, N° 10: "Las modificaciones incorporadas en los artículos 171, 174, 178, 179 y 185 regirán desde el tercer mes contado desde la publicación de la presente ley". The day reads "desde el tercer mes" literally, as the start of the third month, the same count as Ley 21.545's "a contar del décimo segundo mes" (99e-batch14): counted under Código Civil art. 48, the second month ends on 2024-12-24. Read as three months after publication, it would be 2025-01-25 (LeyChile gives 2025-01-24). Credited to Ley 21.713.
- CSan: the 1990-02-21 version (Art. 7) is credited to Ley 18.796: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 18.796, and its art. 12 ("La modificación que la letra a) del artículo 10 de esta ley introduce al artículo 7° del Código Sanitario, comenzará a regir a contar desde la fecha de publicación en el Diario Oficial del decreto con fuerza de ley a que se refiere el artículo anterior") puts this change in force on the publication of that decreto con fuerza de ley: DFL 1 (Ministerio de Salud), "Determina materias que requieren autorización sanitaria expresa", published on that day (LeyChile's records, idNorma 3439).
- CSan: art. 83 (inciso tercero: "o comercial" struck out by Ley 21.770 art. 79 N° 9) takes Ley 21.770's wording on 2025-10-30, the day its own clause gives, not on 2025-10-29 as LeyChile dates it (coordinator task: the acts Ley 21.770 amends follow its own clauses, as its record does): Ley 21.770 (published 2025-09-29), artículo décimo séptimo transitorio: "Lo dispuesto en el artículo 79 numeral 9 [...] entrará en vigencia en el plazo de un mes contado desde la fecha de publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the month, counted under Código Civil art. 48, ends on 2025-10-29. Credited to Ley 21.770.
- CAguas: arts. 41, 129 bis 2, 130 to 134, 139 bis, 140 to 142, 146, 152, 153, 157, 171, 173 and 299 to 301 (Ley 21.770 art. 85 but its N° 18, 19 and 23) take Ley 21.770's wording on 2026-09-30, the day its own clause gives, not on 2026-09-29 as LeyChile dates them (coordinator task: the acts Ley 21.770 amends follow its own clauses, as its record does): Ley 21.770 (published 2025-09-29), artículo décimo noveno transitorio, inciso primero: "El artículo 85, que introduce modificaciones en el Código de Aguas, entrará en vigencia en el plazo de un año contado desde la publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48, ends on 2026-09-29. Credited to Ley 21.770.
- Código Orgánico de Tribunales, Código de Minería, Código Aeronáutico, Código de Justicia Militar, Ley 20.393, Ley 19.880 and Ley 18.010 are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-28 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions: Ley 7.421 of 1943 (25563), Ley 18.248 (29668), Ley 18.916 (30287), Decreto 2.226 of 1944 (18914), Ley 20.393 (1008668), Ley 19.880 (210676) and Ley 18.010 (29438).
- Código de Justicia Militar: LeyChile keeps DL 806 of 1925 (idNorma 6363), which approved the code, in one version only (1926-03-01), so the act starts with Decreto 2.226 of 1944, which fixed the text of the code, as the oldest version available.
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.

- LeyChile version of idNorma 29668 dated 2222-02-02 ("Con Vigencia Diferida por Evento y Derogación Diferida por evento") left out: it waits for an event, not a day.
- CJM: 8 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 19.047's change on 1991-02-14, not on 1991-01-23 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 19.047's own text gives that day (Ley 19.047, promulgated 1991-02-01 and published 1991-02-14, has no clause of its own on when it takes effect (its transitory articles only deal with pending cases), so it is in force from its publication); LeyChile's 1991-01-23, before the law was published, is a date error.
- COT: 1 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 9.555's change on 1950-01-04, not on 1950-01-01 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 9.555 applies it from 1950-01-01, before its publication (Ley 9.555, published 1950-01-04, art. 4: "La presente ley regirá desde el 1.o de Enero de 1950"); publication-day rule: a law's change takes effect no earlier than its publication, its earlier legal start recorded here.
- COT: 1 provisions take LeyChile's wording with DL 1.685 de 1977's change on 1977-02-19, not on 1977-01-25 as LeyChile dates it: DL 1.685 de 1977 applies it from 1977-01-25, before its publication (DL 1.685, published 1977-02-19, art. 2: "El presente decreto ley regirá a contar desde el 25 de Enero de 1977"); publication-day rule: a law's change takes effect no earlier than its publication, its earlier legal start recorded here.
- COT: the changes of Ley 20.322 that LeyChile dates 2013-02-01 (the last regional group) take effect on the days of the law's regional phase-in, line by line; until then each line keeps its earlier wording (added lines are left out): The changes of Ley 20.322 artículo sexto that hold one wording for every Corte de Apelaciones or name Cortes of regions starting on different days (art. 59, the relatores of each Corte; art. 61 incisos 2 and 3 and art. 66 incisos 8 and 9, the salas and relatores for tax and customs matters; art. 69 inciso 2) take effect on 2010-02-02 (Ley 20.322 (published 2009-01-27), artículo 1° transitorio: "Los artículos Primero a Noveno y Undécimo de esta ley entrarán en vigencia en cuatro años contados desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial. Sin perjuicio de lo dispuesto en el inciso anterior, dichos artículos entrarán a regir: En un año, contado desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las siguientes Regiones: XV Región de Arica y Parinacota, I Región de Tarapacá, II Región de Antofagasta, III Región de Atacama"; the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48 from 2009-02-01, ends on 2010-02-01); The changes of Ley 20.322 artículo sexto for the Corte de Apelaciones de Concepción and the Cortes named with it (art. 56 N° 3, Valparaíso and Concepción nineteen members; art. 61 inciso 1, the number of salas of Valparaíso, Concepción, San Miguel and Santiago; art. 66 inciso 7, their tax and customs sala) take effect on 2012-02-02 (Ley 20.322 (published 2009-01-27), artículo 1° transitorio: "En tres años, contados desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las siguientes Regiones: VIII Región del Bío Bío, XIV Región de Los Ríos, X Región de Los Lagos, XI Región de Aysén del General Carlos Ibáñez del Campo"; the three years, counted under Código Civil art. 48 from 2009-02-01, end on 2012-02-01); The changes of Ley 20.322 artículo sexto for the Cortes de Apelaciones de San Miguel and Santiago alone (art. 56 N° 4 and 5, twenty-two and thirty-four members) take effect on 2013-02-02 (Ley 20.322 (published 2009-01-27), artículo 1° transitorio: "Los artículos Primero a Noveno y Undécimo de esta ley entrarán en vigencia en cuatro años contados desde el día primero del mes siguiente al de la fecha de su publicación en el Diario Oficial"; the four years, counted under Código Civil art. 48 from 2009-02-01, end on 2013-02-01 (the V Región and the Región Metropolitana)). A line naming Cortes of regions that start on different days takes the first of those days.
- COT: art. 61 inciso 2 takes Ley 20.322's wording on 2010-02-02 with the law's other changes that hold one wording for every Corte de Apelaciones. Its words "para los efectos de lo dispuesto en los incisos séptimo y noveno del artículo 66" read as art. 66's numbering from 2012-02-02, when Ley 20.322 inserts the new inciso 7 for the Cortes de Apelaciones de Valparaíso, Concepción, San Miguel and Santiago; from 2010-02-02 to 2012-02-01 the incisos they mean stand as art. 66 incisos 7 and 8. That is the law's own text as it stood, so the words are kept as they are.
- COT: the changes of Ley 21.527 art. 56 (arts. 16 bis to 16 quáter, 17, 26 bis and 26 ter: the specialised courts for adolescents) follow the law's regional phase-in, not LeyChile's single day 2026-01-13 (the third stage, uncredited there): Ley 21.527 (published 2023-01-12), artículo primero transitorio: "La presente ley comenzará a regir en forma gradual conforme al cronograma que a continuación se indica: 1.- Transcurridos 12 meses desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las Regiones de Arica y Parinacota, Tarapacá, Antofagasta, Atacama y Coquimbo; 2.- Transcurridos 24 meses desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las Regiones de Maule, Bío Bío, Ñuble, La Araucanía, Los Ríos, Los Lagos, Aysén del General Carlos Ibáñez del Campo y Magallanes y de la Antártica Chilena, y 3.- Transcurridos 36 meses desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las Regiones de Valparaíso, Del Libertador General Bernardo O'Higgins y Metropolitana de Santiago"; counted under Código Civil art. 48, the stages begin on 2024-01-13, 2025-01-13 and 2026-01-13. A line naming courts of one stage only takes that stage: art. 16 bis N° 3 (Concepción) 2025-01-13; N° 1, 2 and 4 (Santiago, San Miguel, Valparaíso) 2026-01-13. Every other line takes the first stage, 2024-01-13 (the shared-line rule, as Leyes 20.322 and 21.057; coordinator decision): art. 16 bis N° 5 names courts of all three stages in one line (Iquique, Antofagasta; Talca, Temuco; Rancagua, San Bernardo, Puente Alto), and N° 6 likewise (Arica, Copiapó, La Serena; Chillán, Valdivia, Puerto Montt, Coyhaique, Punta Arenas; Colina). The COT versions between 2024-01-13 and 2026-01-13 (2024-02-09, 2025-01-14, 2025-02-07: arts. 198 and 451) do not touch these articles, so no change is applied on top of them. Credited to Ley 21.527.
- COT: the 1995-03-01 version (Art. 95 and Art. 99) is credited to Ley 19.374: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 19.374, and its artículo 2° transitorio ("Las modificaciones en la organización y funcionamiento de la Corte Suprema, derivadas de la sustitución de los artículos 95 y 99 del Código Orgánico de Tribunales, entrarán en vigencia el día 1° de marzo siguiente a la fecha de publicación de esta ley") puts these changes in force on that day.
- Ley 19.880: art. 16 (the principle of transparency and publicity), as Ley 20.285 changed it, takes Ley 20.285's wording on 2009-04-21, the day its own artículo transitorio gives, not on its publication, 2008-08-20, as LeyChile dates and credits it (coordinator decision): Ley 20.285 (published 2008-08-20), artículo transitorio: "La presente ley entrará en vigencia ocho meses después de publicada en el Diario Oficial, salvo el artículo 2º transitorio de la Ley de Transparencia de la Función Pública y de Acceso a la Información de la Administración del Estado, que regirá desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial"; the eight months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2009-04-20; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. Only the Transparency Law's artículo 2º transitorio applies from publication, and it changes no other act. Ley 20.285's own text starts on 2009-04-21 too (95-batch6). Credited to Ley 20.285.
- Amendment notes printed inside the law text removed: LeyChile's page text of these older provisions runs its note column (norm, article, Diario Oficial date) into the law's words; only the note fragments are taken out: codigo-justicia-militar art-290; codigo-justicia-militar art-435; codigo-organico-tribunales art-400; codigo-organico-tribunales art-523. Credits dropped where the only change LeyChile shows is the note leaving the text: COT Art. 400 on 1981-03-10 (dl-3637-1981).
- Leyes 18.695, 18.575, 20.000, 17.336, 19.039, 18.045, 20.720 and 16.744 and DFL 1 de 2005 (Salud) are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-28 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions. Where a law was re-enacted as a texto refundido, LeyChile continues it under the new norm and so do we: Ley 18.695 = 30077, then Decreto 662 of 1992 (14962), DFL 2 of 2000 (150973), DFL 1 of 2002 (197482) and DFL 1 of 2006 (251693); Ley 18.575 = 29967, then DFL 1 of 2001 (191865); Ley 19.039 = 30406, then DFL 3 of 2006 (250708) and DFL 4 of 2022 (1179684). The others are one norm each: 235507, 28933, 29472, 1058072, 249177, 28650.
- DFL 1 de 2005 (Salud) fixes in one text DL 2.763 of 1979, Ley 18.933 and Ley 18.469, whose own histories are separate LeyChile norms; the act starts with the DFL as the oldest version available.
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.

- Ley 18.695: art-066 take their ley-19886 wording on 2003-08-30, the day the amending law's own vigencia clause gives (Ley 19.886, art. 39: "La presente ley entrará en vigencia 30 días después de la fecha de su publicación" (published 2003-07-30)), not on 2003-07-30 as LeyChile dates it; the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- DFL 1 2005 (Salud): Art. 60 (Libro II), inciso primero (the Director of the Instituto de Salud Pública, selected through the Alta Dirección Pública system) take effect on 2007-06-08, the day the law's own clause gives (DFL 1 of 2005 (Salud), artículo 6° transitorio: "El inciso primero del artículo 60 del Libro II entrará en vigencia una vez que el Instituto de Salud Pública sea incorporado al Sistema de Alta Dirección Pública, de acuerdo al artículo 14 Transitorio de la Ley N°19.882"; Decreto 85 of 2007 (Hacienda), published 2007-06-08, artículo primero: "Incorpóranse, al Sistema de Alta Dirección Pública [...] el Instituto de Salud Pública"), not on 2006-04-24 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 18.045: art-154, art-155, art-156, art-157, art-158, art-159, art-160, titulos take their ley-20345 wording on 2010-09-07, the day the amending law's own vigencia clause gives (Ley 20.345 (published 2009-06-06), artículo transitorio: "lo dispuesto en los artículos 43 y 44 entrará en vigencia después de quince meses contados desde la fecha de publicación de la presente ley en el Diario Oficial"; the fifteen months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2010-09-06), not on 2010-09-06 as LeyChile dates it; the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 18.575: arts. 13 and 21 take Ley 20.285's wording (artículo segundo N° 1 and 2) on 2009-04-21, not on 2009-04-20 as LeyChile dates it (uncredited there): Ley 20.285 (published 2008-08-20), artículo transitorio: "La presente ley entrará en vigencia ocho meses después de publicada en el Diario Oficial, salvo el artículo 2º transitorio de la Ley de Transparencia de la Función Pública y de Acceso a la Información de la Administración del Estado, que regirá desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial"; the eight months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2009-04-20; the term counted as for Ley 19.300 art. 84 and Ley 20.084 (coordinator decision): it runs from the publication day and ends on the day with the same number (Código Civil art. 48), and the provision takes effect the next day. - Amendment notes printed inside the law text removed: LeyChile's page text of these older provisions runs its note column (norm, article, Diario Oficial date) into the law's words; only the note fragments are taken out: ley-18695 art-029; ley-19039 art-043; ley-19039 art-047; ley-19039 art-052; ley-19039 art-062-bis. - Ley 14.908: from 2000-05-30 its text is article 7 of DFL 1 of 2000 (LeyChile idNorma 172986), which also fixes the Código Civil (its art. 2) and four other laws; only article 7 is read, and versions of the DFL in which only its other articles changed are left out as identical.
- Ley 20.084: its own art. 1 transitorio defers its entry into force ("La presente ley entrará en vigencia dieciocho meses después de su publicación", today's text), and it was amended before that day; the corpus keeps the days LeyChile gives its versions, from publication, so the deferral is in the text, not in the dates. LeyChile's unversioned page for it shows the version waiting for an event (2222-02-02), which is left out; the text in force on 2026-10-28 is the version of 2026-01-13.
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.

- Ley 20.084: first version dated 2007-06-08, the day its own vigencia clause gives ("La presente ley entrará en vigencia dieciocho meses después de su publicación, con excepción de lo dispuesto en las letras a) y c) del artículo 68" (art. 1 transitorio, in the words Ley 20.110 gave it; published 2005-12-07)), not the publication day LeyChile uses; the 4 LeyChile version(s) dated before it are folded into it (Código Civil art. 48 for the count).
- Ley 19.947: first version dated 2004-11-18, the day its own vigencia clause gives ("Esta ley entrará en vigencia seis meses después de su publicación en el Diario Oficial" (artículo final; published 2004-05-17)), not the publication day LeyChile uses; the 1 LeyChile version(s) dated before it are folded into it (Código Civil art. 48 for the count).
- LeyChile version of idNorma 244803 dated 2222-02-02 ("Última Versión") left out: it waits for an event, not a day.
- Ley 18.290: 2 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 21.088's change on 2018-11-11, not on 2016-11-11 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 21.088's own text gives that day (Ley 21.088, published 2018-05-10, artículo primero transitorio: "Esta ley comenzará a regir transcurridos seis meses desde su publicación" (2018-11-11, the day LeyChile gives the rest of the law; its 2016-11-11 has the year wrong)); LeyChile's 2016-11-11, before the law was published, is a date error; art-199, art-200 changed that day by another law as well read the placeholder line from 2016-11-11 to 2018-11-11.
- Ley 18.290: arts. 102 and 196 D on 2005-03-04 are credited to Decreto 1005 EXENTO de 2005 (Justicia, "Reajusta monto de las multas expresadas en pesos establecidas en la ley Nº 18.290", published 2005-03-04), and art. 196 D on 2005-03-12 to Resolución 575 EXENTA de 2005 (Justicia, published 2005-03-12), which rectifies it; both were credited to a "Decreto 1005 de 2005" dated 2005-03-12, taken from the amendment note that names the decreto and the resolución with one Diario Oficial date.
- Ley 18.290: art. 123 on 2005-12-10 is credited to Ley 20.068 (published 2005-12-10), which the LeyChile versions list names for that day; its amendment note reads "LEY 20058 Art. 1º Nº 48 D.O. 10.12.2005", but Ley 20.058 was published 2005-09-26 and does not amend Ley 18.290 there.
- DL 3.500: art. 98 on 2002-03-01 is credited to Ley 19.768 (published 2001-11-07), like the other provisions that change that day; its amendment note reads "LEY 19678 Art. 2° N° 12 D.O. 07.11.2001", but Ley 19.678 was published 2000-05-05.
- Ley 19.300: the 2024-02-01 version (art. 12 letter d)) is credited to Ley 21.455, whose art. 46 N° 4 adds those words; LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, and Ley 21.455's artículo cuarto transitorio puts art. 46 N° 4 in force once the reglamento of its art. 40 is issued: Decreto 30 of 2023 (Ministerio del Medio Ambiente), published 2024-02-01.
- Ley 19.300: The Sistema de Evaluación de Impacto Ambiental (título II, párrafo 2°: arts. 8 to 25 and its heading) take effect on 1997-04-03, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 19.300 (published 1994-03-09), artículo 1° transitorio: "El sistema de evaluación de impacto ambiental que regula el Párrafo 2° del Título II de esta ley, entrará en vigencia una vez publicado en el Diario Oficial el reglamento a que se refiere el artículo 13"; that reglamento, Decreto 30 of 1997 (Ministerio Secretaría General de la Presidencia), "Reglamento del Sistema de Evaluación de Impacto Ambiental", was published on 1997-04-03), not on 1994-03-09 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- DL 3.500: art-068 take their ley-20255 wording on 2008-07-01, the day the amending law's own vigencia clause gives (Ley 20.255 (published 2008-03-17), artículo primero transitorio: "Las disposiciones del Título I de la presente ley entrarán en vigencia a contar del 1 de julio de 2008"), not on 2008-03-17 as LeyChile dates it; the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- DL 3.500: Título XVI (the Consejo Técnico de Inversiones: arts. 167 to 170 and its heading) take effect on 2008-04-01, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 20.255 (published 2008-03-17), artículo trigésimo segundo transitorio: "El Título XVI del decreto ley N° 3.500, de 1980, incorporado por el número 85 del artículo 91, comenzará a regir el primer día del mes siguiente al de la publicación de esta ley"; LeyChile's own note on these articles says the same), not on 2008-10-01 as LeyChile dates the text; they carry LeyChile's wording of 2008-10-01.
- DL 3.500: the 2008-07-01 version is credited to Ley 20.255: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, every change on it carries a Ley 20.255 note, and its artículo primero transitorio (título I, 1 July 2008) puts them in force that day.
- DL 3.500: the 2008-10-01 version is credited to Ley 20.255: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, every change on it carries a Ley 20.255 note, and its artículos vigésimo octavo and trigésimo segundo transitorios (título III párrafo 4° and título V, the first day of the seventh month after publication) puts them in force that day.
- DL 3.500: the 2009-01-01 version is credited to Ley 20.255: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, every change on it carries a Ley 20.255 note, and its artículo cuadragésimo sexto transitorio (art. 91 N° 8, 1 January of the next year) puts them in force that day.
- DL 3.500: the 2009-03-01 version is credited to Ley 20.255: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, every change on it carries a Ley 20.255 note, and its artículo vigésimo segundo transitorio (art. 91 N° 10 and N° 64 g), the first day of the twelfth month after publication) puts them in force that day.
- DL 3.500: the 2009-07-01 version is credited to Ley 20.255: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, every change on it carries a Ley 20.255 note, and its artículo décimo quinto transitorio (art. 38 N° 6 b), 7 a) and c), 8 a), 9 a), 10 and 12, 1 July 2009) puts them in force that day.
- Ley 19.300: Art. 3 (the duty to repair environmental damage) take effect on 1994-06-08, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 19.300 (published 1994-03-09), artículo 6° transitorio: "Lo dispuesto en el artículo 3.- se aplicará a contar de los noventa días siguientes a la publicación de la presente ley" (the ninety days end on 1994-06-07, Código Civil art. 48)), not on 1994-03-09 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 19.300: Art. 84 (the Comisión Regional del Medio Ambiente of the Región Metropolitana succeeds the Comisión Especial de Descontaminación de la Región Metropolitana) take effect on 1996-03-10, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 19.300 (published 1994-03-09), artículo 2° transitorio: "Una vez constituida la Comisión Regional del Medio Ambiente de la Región Metropolitana o vencido el plazo señalado en el inciso anterior, entrará en vigencia el artículo 84 de esta ley"; that plazo is "de dos años contado desde la fecha de publicación de la presente ley", which ends on 1996-03-09 (Código Civil art. 48)), not on 1994-03-09 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days. No published norm dates the constitution of that Comisión Regional, so the article is shown from the latest day the law allows; it may have applied earlier, from the day the Comisión was constituted..
- DL 3.500: the 2018-05-01 version (Art. 94 and Art. 94 bis) is credited to Ley 20.956: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 20.956, and its artículo cuarto transitorio ("las modificaciones introducidas por los numerales 4 y 5 del precitado artículo comenzarán a regir el primer día del decimonoveno mes siguiente al de su publicación en el Diario Oficial", of its art. 5) puts these changes in force on that day.

- Ley 18.290: the 2025-11-28 version (Art. 63) is credited to Ley 21.659: LeyChile's versions list names no norm for that day, its notes name Ley 21.659, and its artículo primero transitorio ("Esta ley entrará en vigencia seis meses después de la publicación en el Diario Oficial del último de sus reglamentos complementarios"; of the two it calls for, the reglamento of the law and the reglamento on eventos masivos, the last published is Decreto 209 of 2025 (Ministerio de Seguridad Pública), "Aprueba reglamento de seguridad privada de la ley N° 21.659", 2025-05-27; the six months run to the end of 2025-11-27, Código Civil art. 48) puts this change in force on that day.
- Ley 19.968: art. 8 N° 10 bis, added by Ley 20.084 art. 68 b), takes effect on 2007-06-08, not on 2006-12-07 as LeyChile dates it (uncredited there): Ley 20.084 (published 2005-12-07), artículo 1° transitorio, as Ley 20.110 (published 2006-06-01) worded it: "La presente ley entrará en vigencia dieciocho meses después de su publicación, con excepción de lo dispuesto en las letras a) y c) del artículo 68"; art. 68 b) adds this numeral, and the eighteen months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2007-06-07. Ley 20.110 replaced "seis meses" with "dieciocho meses" before the first term ran out; LeyChile's day, twelve months after publication, is neither term. Ley 20.084's own text starts on 2007-06-08 here too. Credited to Ley 20.084.
- Ley 20.084: a regional phase-in, as Ley 21.057 (coordinator decision): LeyChile dates the changes of Ley 21.527 art. 55 on 2026-01-13, the third stage, uncredited, and only N° 15, 17 and 18 on 2024-01-13. No line of Ley 20.084 as amended names a region, so every changed line holds one wording for the whole country and takes effect on the first stage, 2024-01-13 (Ley 21.527 (published 2023-01-12), artículo primero transitorio: "La presente ley comenzará a regir en forma gradual conforme al cronograma que a continuación se indica: 1.- Transcurridos 12 meses desde su publicación en el Diario Oficial, en las Regiones de Arica y Parinacota, Tarapacá, Antofagasta, Atacama y Coquimbo; 2.- Transcurridos 24 meses [...]; 3.- Transcurridos 36 meses [...], en las Regiones de Valparaíso, Del Libertador General Bernardo O'Higgins y Metropolitana de Santiago"; the twelve months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2024-01-12); artículo sexto transitorio puts N° 15, 17 (but art. 25 bis) and 18 in force nationwide on that day as well. The second and third stages (2025-01-13, 2026-01-13) bring no line of their own. Credited to Ley 21.527.
- Ley 18.290: the 2021-05-22 version (arts. 39, 39 bis, 41, 42, 45, 46, 51 and 53, changed by Ley 21.180 art. 5) keeps LeyChile's day, uncredited: Ley 21.180 (published 2019-11-11), artículo segundo transitorio, applies the law "gradualmente, de acuerdo a las fases establecidas en el decreto con fuerza de ley N° 1, de 2020, del Ministerio Secretaría General de la Presidencia". The date that decreto con fuerza de ley gives could not be read, so the day is not checked against the law's own text.
- Amendment notes printed inside the law text removed: LeyChile's page text of these older provisions runs its note column (norm, article, Diario Oficial date) into the law's words; only the note fragments are taken out: dl-3500 art-068; dl-3500 art-079; dl-3500 art-092; dl-3500 art-151; ley-18290 art-175.
- Leyes 19.537, 18.101, 20.285, 18.700, 20.584, 19.733, 20.529 and 21.091 are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-28 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions: 81505, 29526, 276363, Ley 18.700 = 30082 then DFL 2 of 2017 (1108229), 1039348, 186049, 1028635 and 1118991.
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.

- Ley 19.537: repealed on 2022-04-13 by Ley 21.442 (LeyChile: derogado, fecha_derogacion 2022-04-13); a version that day removes 62 of its 63 provisions, credited to that law, and art. 36 follows on 2025-07-10, when Ley 21.442's art. 43 applies; see its own line.
- Ley 18.700: 9 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 18.809's change on 1989-06-15, not on 1989-05-26 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 18.809's own text gives that day (Ley 18.809, promulgated 1989-06-14 and published 1989-06-15, has no clause of its own on when it takes effect, so it is in force from its publication); LeyChile's 1989-05-26, before the law was published, is a date error.
- Ley 20.529: 2 provisions take LeyChile's wording with Ley 21.827's change on 2026-08-12, not on 2026-07-01 as LeyChile dates it: Ley 21.827 applies it from 2026-07-01, before its publication (Ley 21.827, published 2026-08-12, artículo transitorio: "Los artículos 2 y 3 entrarán en vigor a contar de la fecha de entrada en vigencia de la ley N° 21.809" (art. 2 amends Ley 20.529, art. 3 Ley 19.070; LeyChile dates Ley 21.809 2026-07-01)); publication-day rule: a law's change takes effect no earlier than its publication, its earlier legal start recorded here; art-061 changed that day by another law as well read the placeholder line from 2026-07-01 to 2026-08-12.
- Ley 21.091: Art. 119, numerals 1, 2, 4 and 5 (the Escuela de Gendarmería de Chile in decreto con fuerza de ley N° 2 of 2009) take effect on 2026-05-05, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo cuadragésimo cuarto transitorio: "Los numerales 1, 2, 4 y 5 del artículo 119 entrarán en vigencia una vez que la Escuela de Gendarmería de Chile adecue sus requisitos de ingreso, planes y programas a las normativas de educación superior. Este requisito se verificará fundadamente a través de un decreto del Ministerio de Justicia y Derechos Humanos"; Decreto 1.027 exento of 2026 (Justicia y Derechos Humanos), which verifies that adaptation, was published on 2026-05-05), not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 21.091: Art. 81 numerals 15 to 18, 21, 23, 24, 27, 38 and 46 to 49 (changes to Ley 20.129) take effect on the days the law's own clauses give, not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text: Art. 81 N° 46 to 49 (capítulo III of Ley 20.129, arts. 49 to 54) take effect on 2019-08-01 (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo vigésimo primero transitorio: "las disposiciones del artículo 81 de la presente ley que modifican el capítulo III de la ley N° 20.129 entrarán en vigencia una vez que inicie su funcionamiento la Subsecretaría de Educación Superior"; DFL 3 of 2019 (Educación, published 2019-07-04), art. 5, starts the Subsecretaría on "el 1 de agosto del 2019"); Art. 81 N° 15 letters a) and c) (compulsory institutional accreditation), N° 17, 21, 23, 24 and 27 take effect on 2020-01-01 (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo vigésimo primero transitorio: "Los numerales 15, en lo relativo a la obligatoriedad de la acreditación, 17, 21, 23, en cuanto a la acreditación basada en niveles, 24 y 27 del artículo 81 de esta ley, que modifica la ley Nº 20.129, entrarán en vigencia a contar del 1 de enero de 2020"); Art. 81 N° 15 letters b) and d) (integral accreditation and the intentional sample), N° 16 and 18 take effect on 2023-10-01 (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo vigésimo primero transitorio: "Los numerales 15, en cuanto a la integralidad y muestra intencionada, 16 y 18 entrarán en vigencia junto con los nuevos criterios y estándares, según lo dispuesto en el inciso cuarto del artículo vigésimo segundo transitorio"; artículo vigésimo segundo transitorio, inciso cuarto: "Los nuevos criterios y estándares de calidad sólo serán aplicables a aquellos procesos de acreditación iniciados una vez trascurridos veinticuatro meses desde la fecha de su publicación"; the Comisión Nacional de Acreditación published them on 2021-09-30 (Resoluciones exentas 252 to 258 of 2021), so from 2023-10-01); Art. 81 N° 38 (new art. 30 of Ley 20.129, with the inciso Ley 21.186 added to it in 2019) take effect on 2024-12-31 (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo trigésimo transitorio: "El nuevo artículo 30 de la ley N° 20.129, incorporado por el numeral 38) del artículo 81, entrará en vigencia a partir del 31 de diciembre de 2024"). N° 23 arrives whole on 2020-01-01 ("en cuanto a la acreditación basada en niveles": its new art. 20 is the accreditation by levels), as LeyChile dates Ley 20.129 art. 20; artículo vigésimo cuarto transitorio (the duty to accredit medicine, dentistry and doctoral programmes from 2020) limits whom N° 31 and 41 reach and is not staged. The other numerals and provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 21.091: Título V párrafo 4° (art. 102, the regulation of vacancies) and its heading take effect on 2021-01-01, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo cuadragésimo segundo transitorio: "Lo dispuesto en el párrafo 4° del título V entrará en vigencia el año siguiente a la entrada en vigencia del Sistema de Acceso regulado en el párrafo 3° del título I"; artículo cuarto transitorio: the Sistema de Acceso "entrará en funcionamiento a partir del año 2020 para los procesos de admisión del año 2021"; so from 2021), not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 21.091: Art. 120 numerals 1 to 4, 6, 7 letters b) to f) and 8 to 18 (changes to Ley 20.800) take effect on the days the law's own clauses give, not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text: Art. 120 N° 1 to 4, 6, 7 letters b) to f) and 8 to 18 (the Superintendencia de Educación Superior takes over the Ministry's powers under Ley 20.800) take effect on 2019-05-06 (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo décimo quinto transitorio: "Las modificaciones establecidas en el artículo 120 entrarán en vigencia desde la fecha de entrada en funcionamiento de la Superintendencia de Educación Superior, de acuerdo a lo establecido en el número 6) del artículo décimo primero transitorio, a excepción de las indicadas en los números 5) y 7) letra a) del señalado artículo 120"; DFL 2 of 2019 (Educación), art. 6: "la fecha de iniciación de funciones de la Superintendencia de Educación Superior será la de publicación del presente decreto con fuerza de ley", published 2019-05-06). N° 5 and 7 letter a) are in force from publication, as the clause says. The other numerals and provisions keep LeyChile's days.
- Ley 21.091: Art. 36 (full accounting under the accounting rules the Superintendencia defines, and external audit) take effect on 2021-12-23, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo décimo sexto transitorio: "Las obligaciones establecidas en el artículo 36 comenzarán a regir un año después a partir de la fecha en que la Superintendencia defina las respectivas normas contables"; the Superintendencia first defined them, for the state institutions of higher education, by the joint Resolución N° 284 of the Superintendencia de Educación Superior and N° 4848 of the Contraloría General de la República of 22 December 2020, "que aprueba la normativa contable para las Instituciones de Educación Superior del Estado" (recited in their Resolución exenta N° 432 and N° 2303 of 2023); the year ends on 2021-12-22 (Código Civil art. 48)), not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days. The accounting rules for the institutions that do not belong to the State were defined later (Resolución exenta N° 510 of 2021 of the Superintendencia), so for them the obligations began later; the rules themselves apply their formats from the next accounting period (1 January 2022 for the state institutions, 1 January 2023 for the others). Those first accounting periods are when the norms' formats apply, not the day the article takes effect, which the law counts one year from the day the rules are defined..
- Ley 19.537: art. 36 (insurance against fire, the emergency plan, inspections and access to units) stays in force after the rest of the law is repealed on 2022-04-13 and is repealed on 2025-07-10, when Ley 21.442's art. 43 applies. Ley 21.442 art. 100 repeals Ley 19.537 "sin perjuicio de lo dispuesto en el artículo 5° transitorio de la presente ley", and that article keeps art. 36 in force meanwhile (Ley 21.442 (published 2022-04-13), artículo 5° transitorio: "La obligación contemplada en el artículo 43, relacionada con la contratación de un seguro colectivo contra incendio, será exigible una vez transcurridos seis meses desde la publicación del reglamento de esta ley. En el tiempo intermedio, seguirá vigente lo dispuesto en el artículo 36 de la ley N° 19.537"; the reglamento, Decreto 7 of 2023 (Vivienda y Urbanismo), "Aprueba reglamento de la ley N° 21.442 que aprueba nueva ley de copropiedad inmobiliaria", was published on 2025-01-09, and the six months, counted under Código Civil art. 48, end on 2025-07-09).
- Ley 21.091: Art. 83 letter d) (the equitable-access policies and support programmes required for institutional funding for free tuition) take effect on 2020-08-02, the day the law's own clause gives (Ley 21.091 (published 2018-05-29), artículo cuadragésimo transitorio, inciso final: "Respecto del requisito establecido en la letra d) del artículo 83, éste entrará en vigencia un año después de la entrada en funcionamiento de la Subsecretaría"; the Subsecretaría de Educación Superior started on 2019-08-01 (DFL 3 of 2019, Educación, art. 5), and the year, counted under Código Civil art. 48, ends on 2020-08-01), not on 2018-05-29 as LeyChile dates the text; until then they are held out of the text and the other provisions keep LeyChile's days. Letters a) and b) are exigible from publication and letter c) two years after the Sistema de Acceso starts (the same article); those are terms for meeting requirements already in force, so they stay in the text..
- Ley 21.091: artículos décimo séptimo and cuadragésimo transitorios keep arts. 37 and 83 on publication, 2018-05-29, as LeyChile dates them: "Las obligaciones de informar que establece el artículo 37 a las instituciones de educación superior, serán exigibles en el plazo de un año contado desde la publicación de la presente ley" and "la exigencia de estar adscritas al Sistema de Acceso regulado en la letra c) del artículo 83, será exigible transcurridos dos años desde que aquél entre en funcionamiento" (with an interim admission requirement meanwhile) give periods for complying with duties the text already sets, not a later day for the text. Only art. 83 letter d), which the same article puts "en vigencia un año después de la entrada en funcionamiento de la Subsecretaría", is held; see its own line.
- DL 824 and DL 825 of 1974 and Leyes 21.325, 19.886, 18.840, 20.066, 18.314, 17.798, 20.609 and 19.799 are parsed by the same rules as the Constitution, from the LeyChile norms verified on 2026-10-29 against LeyChile's list of each norm's consolidated texts and versions: 6368, 6369, 1158549, 213004, 30216, 242648, 29731, Ley 17.798 = 29291 then Decreto 400 of 1978 (13031, texto refundido), 1042092 and 196640. Retrieved from LeyChile on 2026-10-29; the repository's docs date stays 2026-10-28, the day the other acts were retrieved.
- Ley 20.066: its art. 25 says "La presente ley comenzará a regir el 1 de Octubre de 2005", a day before its publication in the Diario Oficial (2005-10-07); a law cannot bind before it is published (Código Civil arts. 7 and 9), so its first version keeps LeyChile's day, 2005-10-07.
- Ley 21.325: "Esta ley entrará en vigencia una vez publicado su reglamento" (art. 11 transitorio); LeyChile already dates its first version from that day, 2022-02-12, not from publication (2021-04-20).
- A version dated D is anachronistic when its amendment notes name a norm published after D (by Diario Oficial date, or by a Ley number at least that of a Ley published after D). Where LeyChile's "Texto Original" of a code holds no article text (only a PDF link), its later old versions are reconstructions, and the code starts at the oldest version from which no version is anachronistic: none. The first version is then the oldest version available, credited to the publisher.
